
<!DOCTYPE html> <html lang="en"><head>  <script type="text/javascript">
        (function () {
            var HS_SCRIPT_ID = 'hs-script-loader';
            var HS_SCRIPT_SRC = 'https://js-na1.hs-scripts.com/897267.js';
            // Align with OneTrust HubSpot guidance + GTM HubSpot tag (ad_storage / C0004).
            var HS_CONSENT_GROUP = 'C0004';

            function isPpcPage() {
                try {
                    return /^\/(tr\/)?ppc(\/|$)/i.test(window.location.pathname || '');
                } catch (e) {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            function getActiveGroups() {
                return String(window.OnetrustActiveGroups || window.OptanonActiveGroups || '');
            }

            function hasTargetingConsent() {
                // OneTrust format is typically ",C0001,C0004," — require exact group token.
                var groups = ',' + getActiveGroups().replace(/\s/g, '') + ',';
                return groups.indexOf(',' + HS_CONSENT_GROUP + ',') > -1;
            }

            function pushHubSpotDoNotTrack(allowTrack) {
                window._hsq = window._hsq || [];
                if (allowTrack) {
                    window._hsq.push(['doNotTrack', { track: true }]);
                } else {
                    window._hsq.push(['doNotTrack']);
                }
            }

            function removeHubSpotChatDom() {
                var container = document.getElementById('hubspot-messages-iframe-container');
                if (container && container.parentNode) {
                    container.parentNode.removeChild(container);
                }
                var loader = document.getElementById('hubspot-messages-loader');
                if (loader && loader.parentNode) {
                    loader.parentNode.removeChild(loader);
                }
            }

            function disableHubSpotChat() {
                pushHubSpotDoNotTrack(false);
                removeHubSpotChatDom();
                try {
                    if (window.HubSpotConversations && typeof window.HubSpotConversations.widget === 'object') {
                        if (typeof window.HubSpotConversations.widget.remove === 'function') {
                            window.HubSpotConversations.widget.remove();
                        } else if (typeof window.HubSpotConversations.widget.close === 'function') {
                            window.HubSpotConversations.widget.close();
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e) {}
            }

            function loadHubSpotChatScript() {
                if (document.getElementById(HS_SCRIPT_ID)) {
                    pushHubSpotDoNotTrack(true);
                    return;
                }

                var script = document.createElement('script');
                script.id = HS_SCRIPT_ID;
                script.type = 'text/javascript';
                script.async = true;
                script.defer = true;
                script.src = HS_SCRIPT_SRC;
                (document.head || document.body || document.documentElement).appendChild(script);
                pushHubSpotDoNotTrack(true);
            }

            window.ctSyncHubSpotChatConsent = function ctSyncHubSpotChatConsent() {
                if (isPpcPage()) {
                    disableHubSpotChat();
                    return;
                }

                if (hasTargetingConsent()) {
                    loadHubSpotChatScript();
                    return;
                }

                // Reject All / Targeting off: do not load; stop tracking if already present.
                disableHubSpotChat();
            };

            // OneTrust calls this on SDK init (persisted consent) and after consent changes.
            window.OptanonWrapper = function OptanonWrapper() {
                window.ctSyncHubSpotChatConsent();
            };

            window.addEventListener('OneTrustGroupsUpdated', function () {
                window.ctSyncHubSpotChatConsent();
            });

            // Prefer OT API when available (fires on user change after SDK ready).
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
                try {
                    if (window.OneTrust && typeof window.OneTrust.OnConsentChanged === 'function') {
                        window.OneTrust.OnConsentChanged(function () {
                            window.ctSyncHubSpotChatConsent();
                        });
                    }
                } catch (e) {}
            });
        })();

$(document).ready(function () {
    var slickOpts = {
        arrows: false,
        dots: false,
        infinite: true,
        autoplay: true,
        variableWidth: true,
        slidesToShow: 1,
        slidesToScroll: 1,
        speed: 4000,
        autoplaySpeed: 0,
        cssEase: 'linear',
        waitForAnimate: false,
        pauseOnHover: false,
        pauseOnFocus: false,
        draggable: false,
        swipe: false,
        touchMove: false
    };

    function hydrateImages($root) {
        $root.find('img').each(function () {
            var src = this.getAttribute('src') || this.getAttribute('data-src');
            if (src) {
                this.setAttribute('src', src);
            }
            // Kill legacy left-float / margin overrides from theme CSS & inline hacks
            this.style.setProperty('float', 'none', 'important');
            this.style.setProperty('margin', '0', 'important');
            this.style.setProperty('width', 'auto', 'important');
            this.style.setProperty('height', 'auto', 'important');
            this.style.setProperty('display', 'block', 'important');
            this.style.setProperty('object-fit', 'contain', 'important');
            this.style.setProperty('object-position', 'center center', 'important');
        });
    }

    function wrapLogos($el) {
        $el.children('img').each(function () {
            $(this).wrap('<div class="customer-logo-cell"></div>');
        });
    }

    function initOne($el) {
        if (!$el.length || $el.hasClass('slick-initialized')) {
            return;
        }
        hydrateImages($el);
        wrapLogos($el);
        $el.slick(slickOpts);
        hydrateImages($el);
        $el.css('opacity', '1');
        $el.find('img').css('opacity', '1');
        $el.slick('setPosition');
    }

    function initVisible() {
        $('.customer_logo_slider:visible').each(function () {
            initOne($(this));
        });
        $('.customer__dummy').fadeOut();
    }

    hydrateImages($('.customer_logo_slider'));

    var $visibleImgs = $('.customer_logo_slider:visible img');
    var loaded = 0;
    var total = $visibleImgs.length;
    var started = false;

    function maybeInit() {
        loaded += 1;
        if (!started && loaded >= total) {
            started = true;
            initVisible();
        }
    }

    if (total === 0) {
        initVisible();
    } else {
        $visibleImgs.each(function () {
            if (this.complete && this.naturalWidth) {
                maybeInit();
            } else {
                $(this).one('load error', maybeInit);
            }
        });
        setTimeout(function () {
            if (!started) {
                started = true;
                initVisible();
            }
        }, 2500);
    }

    $(window).on('load', function () {
        initVisible();
        $('.customer_logo_slider.slick-initialized:visible').slick('setPosition');
    });
});

(function () {
  var formId = "643700df-e938-4d6f-85dc-734ee3c7a2e7";
  if (!formId) return;

  function initMainDemoPopupForm() {
    if (typeof window.initCleverTapForm !== 'function') return;

    window.__ctMainDemoPopupInit = window.__ctMainDemoPopupInit || {};
    if (window.__ctMainDemoPopupInit[formId]) return;

    var alreadyRegistered = window.__ctHubspotSettingsByFormId &&
      window.__ctHubspotSettingsByFormId[formId];
    if (alreadyRegistered) {
      window.__ctMainDemoPopupInit[formId] = true;
      return;
    }

    window.initCleverTapForm({
      formId: formId,
      checkValidDomain: true,
      ctEvent: true,
      formName: 'Demo Form',
      isModal: true,
      redirectUrl: '/live-product-demo/thank-you/',
      form_prop: {
        lifecyclestage: 'marketingqualifiedlead',
        ct_lead_source: 'Demo Signup'
      }
    });

    window.__ctMainDemoPopupInit[formId] = true;
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initMainDemoPopupForm);
  } else {
    initMainDemoPopupForm();
  }

  // Re-check on popup open in case ct-hubspot loads late.
  document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
    if (event.target.closest('.open-modal[data-modal="signUpP"]')) {
      setTimeout(initMainDemoPopupForm, 0);
    }
  });
})();

// Allow only digits for legacy fields that attach this handler
function IsNumeric(e) {
  const k = e.which ? e.which : e.keyCode;
  if ([8,9,37,39,46].includes(k)) return true;      // backspace, tab, arrows, delete
  return (k >= 48 && k <= 57);                      // digits
}

(function () {
  // Prefer #phone; fall back to #sdphone (ES/PT forms)
  var input = document.getElementById('phone') || document.getElementById('sdphone');
  if (!input || !window.intlTelInput) return;

  // ---- Timezone → Country mapping (better than defaulting to India) ----
  var tz = (window.intlOpts && window.intlOpts.timeZone) ||
           (Intl.DateTimeFormat && Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone) ||
           "";

  var tzCountryMap = {
    // India & nearby
    "Asia/Kolkata": "IN",
    // Middle East / APAC
    "Asia/Dubai": "AE",
    "Asia/Qatar": "QA",
    "Asia/Kuwait": "KW",
    "Asia/Riyadh": "SA",
    "Asia/Singapore": "SG",
    "Asia/Jakarta": "ID",
    "Asia/Bangkok": "TH",
    "Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh": "VN",
    "Asia/Tokyo": "JP",
    "Asia/Seoul": "KR",
    "Asia/Hong_Kong": "HK",
    "Asia/Shanghai": "CN",
    // Europe
    "Europe/London": "GB",
    "Europe/Dublin": "IE",
    "Europe/Berlin": "DE",
    "Europe/Paris": "FR",
    "Europe/Madrid": "ES",
    "Europe/Rome": "IT",
    "Europe/Amsterdam": "NL",
    "Europe/Stockholm": "SE",
    "Europe/Copenhagen": "DK",
    "Europe/Zurich": "CH",
    // North America
    "America/New_York": "US",
    "America/Chicago": "US",
    "America/Denver": "US",
    "America/Los_Angeles": "US",
    "America/Toronto": "CA",
    "America/Vancouver": "CA",
    "America/Mexico_City": "MX",
    // South America
    "America/Sao_Paulo": "BR",
    "America/Bogota": "CO",
    "America/Lima": "PE",
    // Australia / NZ / Africa
    "Australia/Sydney": "AU",
    "Australia/Melbourne": "AU",
    "Australia/Brisbane": "AU",
    "Pacific/Auckland": "NZ",
    "Africa/Johannesburg": "ZA"
  };

  var isoFromTz = tzCountryMap[tz] || "";
  var hasTzMatch = !!isoFromTz;

  // ---- Build options for intl-tel-input ----
  var itiOptions = {
    separateDialCode: true,
    utilsScript: 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/intl-tel-input@17.0.19/build/js/utils.js'
  };

  if (hasTzMatch) {
    // Use timezone-derived country directly (more reliable than geoIP)
    itiOptions.initialCountry = isoFromTz.toLowerCase();
  } else {
    // Fall back to geoIpLookup if timezone not in map
    // IMPORTANT: Don't default to India - use geoIP result or US as last resort
    itiOptions.initialCountry = "auto";
    itiOptions.geoIpLookup = function (cb) {
      fetch('https://ipinfo.io/json', {cache:'no-store', timeout: 5000})
        .then(function(r) {
          if (!r.ok) throw new Error('Network error');
          return r.json();
        })
        .then(function(d) {
          // Use geoIP country if available, otherwise use US (not India)
          cb((d && d.country) || 'US');
        })
        .catch(function() {
          // If geoIP fails, use US as default (not India)
          // This prevents UAE submissions from defaulting to India
          cb('US');
        });
    };
  }

  // Init intl-tel-input
  var iti = window.intlTelInput(input, itiOptions);

  // ---- grab all fields we may need to touch ----
  var form = input.form || document.querySelector('form[id^="ctForm_"]');
  var hsISD = (form && form.querySelector('input[name="phone_number_country"]')) || document.getElementById('phone_number_country');

  var countryInput  = document.getElementById('country');
  var countryHidden = document.querySelector('.country_hidden');

  var visiblePhone = document.getElementById('phone');
  var mirrorPhone  = document.getElementById('sdphone');
  if (visiblePhone && mirrorPhone) {
    mirrorPhone.removeAttribute('name'); // prevent duplicate "phone"
    var syncMirror = function(){ mirrorPhone.value = visiblePhone.value; };
    ['input','change','blur'].forEach(function(ev){ visiblePhone.addEventListener(ev, syncMirror); });
  }

  function writeAll() {
    var c = iti.getSelectedCountryData() || {};
    var dial = c.dialCode ? ('+' + c.dialCode) : '';
    var name = (c.name || '').replace(/\s*\(.*\)\s*$/, ''); // strip local-language suffix
    var e164 = iti.getNumber(); // +<code><number>

    // 1) HubSpot property: Phone Number Country -> "+ISD Country"
    if (hsISD) {
      var combined = dial && name ? (dial + ' ' + name) : (dial || name || '');
      hsISD.value = combined;
      hsISD.setAttribute('value', combined);
    }

    // 2) Optional: keep readable country elsewhere
    if (countryInput) {
      countryInput.value = name;
      countryInput.setAttribute('value', name);
    }
    if (countryHidden) countryHidden.value = name;

    // 3) Ensure the posted "phone" is E.164
    document.querySelectorAll('input[name="phone"]').forEach(function(el){
      el.value = e164;
      el.setAttribute('value', e164);
    });

    // 4) Fill optional breakout fields if present
    var dEl  = document.getElementById('phone_dial_code');
    var isoEl= document.getElementById('phone_country_iso2');
    var nmEl = document.getElementById('phone_country_name');
    var eEl  = document.getElementById('phone_e164');
    if (dEl)  { dEl.value = dial; dEl.setAttribute('value', dial); }
    if (isoEl){ isoEl.value = (c.iso2 || '').toUpperCase(); isoEl.setAttribute('value', isoEl.value); }
    if (nmEl) { nmEl.value = name; nmEl.setAttribute('value', name); }
    if (eEl)  { eEl.value = e164; eEl.setAttribute('value', e164); }
  }

  // initial populate + react to country change / blur
  writeAll();
  input.addEventListener('countrychange', writeAll);
  input.addEventListener('blur', writeAll);

  // CRITICAL: before any submission/CTA click, rewrite again (covers AJAX flows)
  // This MUST run synchronously before the form handler reads the values
  function beforeSubmit(){
    // Force synchronous update of all fields
    writeAll();
    // Double-check: ensure values are set on the actual DOM elements
    // This prevents race conditions where form handler reads before writeAll completes
    setTimeout(function() {
      writeAll(); // One more time to be absolutely sure
    }, 0);
  }
  
  // Attach to form submit event (capture phase - runs first)
  if (form) {
    form.addEventListener('submit', beforeSubmit, true);
  }
  
  // Attach to button clicks (capture phase - runs first, before other handlers)
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var target = e.target;
    var button = target.closest('#schedule_demo,[type="submit"],[name="submit"]');
    if (button) {
      // CRITICAL: Call writeAll() immediately and synchronously
      // This ensures country data is captured before ct-hubspot.js reads it
      writeAll();
      // Also trigger it one more time after a microtask to catch any async updates
      setTimeout(writeAll, 0);
    }
  }, true); // Use capture phase to run before other handlers
})();

/* Country datalist: dedupe helper (kept for compatibility if you later show the field) */
(function () {
  const DL_ID = 'countrylist';
  const INIT_ATTR = 'data-countrylist-init';

  window.buildCountryListOnce = function (countries) {
    const dl = document.getElementById(DL_ID);
    if (!dl || dl.getAttribute(INIT_ATTR) === '1') return;
    dl.innerHTML = '';
    const seen = new Set();
    (countries || []).forEach(c => {
      const name = (typeof c === 'string' ? c : c.name || '').trim();
      if (!name) return;
      const key = name.toLowerCase();
      if (seen.has(key)) return;
      seen.add(key);
      const opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = name;
      dl.appendChild(opt);
    });
    dl.setAttribute(INIT_ATTR, '1');
  };

  function dedupeExisting() {
    const dl = document.getElementById(DL_ID);
    if (!dl) return;
    const seen = new Set();
    Array.from(dl.querySelectorAll('option')).forEach(opt => {
      const key = (opt.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!key) { opt.remove(); return; }
      if (seen.has(key)) opt.remove();
      else seen.add(key);
    });
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', dedupeExisting);
  } else {
    dedupeExisting();
  }

  const dl = document.getElementById(DL_ID);
  if (dl) {
    new MutationObserver(dedupeExisting).observe(dl, { childList: true });
  }
})();
</script><script>
// Mobile-only: if you later show the datalist country field, this swaps it for a <select> on touch devices.
(function(){
  var isTouch = ('ontouchstart' in window) || (navigator.maxTouchPoints > 0) ||
                (window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches);
  if (!isTouch) return;

  function isVisible(el){ return !!(el && el.offsetParent !== null && el.getClientRects().length); }

  function buildMobileSelect(fromInput){
    if (!fromInput || !isVisible(fromInput)) return;
    var listId = fromInput.getAttribute('list'); if (!listId) return;
    var dl = document.getElementById(listId); if (!dl) return;

    var next = fromInput.nextElementSibling;
    var sel = (next && next.tagName === 'SELECT' && next.dataset.fromDatalist === listId) ? next : null;

    if (!sel) {
      sel = document.createElement('select');
      sel.className = fromInput.className;
      sel.id = (fromInput.id ? fromInput.id + '_select' : '');
      sel.dataset.fromDatalist = listId;
      sel.style.width = '100%';

      fromInput.style.display = 'none';
      fromInput.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      fromInput.setAttribute('tabindex','-1');

      fromInput.parentNode.insertBefore(sel, fromInput.nextSibling);

      sel.addEventListener('change', function(){
        var v = sel.value;
        fromInput.value = v;
        fromInput.setAttribute('value', v);
        var hidden = fromInput.parentNode.querySelector('.country_hidden') || document.querySelector('.country_hidden');
        if (hidden) hidden.value = v;
        try { fromInput.dispatchEvent(new InputEvent('input', { bubbles: true })); }
        catch(e) { fromInput.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); }
        fromInput.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
      });
    }

    function repopulateFromDatalist(){
      var opts = dl.querySelectorAll('option');
      var hadSelection = sel.value || fromInput.value;
      var placeholderText = fromInput.getAttribute('placeholder') || 'Select Country';

      sel.innerHTML = '';
      var ph = document.createElement('option');
      ph.value = '';
      ph.disabled = true;
      ph.textContent = opts.length ? placeholderText : 'Loading countries…';
      if (!fromInput.value && !hadSelection) ph.selected = true;
      sel.appendChild(ph);

      var seen = new Set();
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(opts, function(opt){
        var val = (opt.value || '').trim(); if (!val) return;
        var key = val.toLowerCase(); if (seen.has(key)) return;
        seen.add(key);
        var o = document.createElement('option');
        o.value = val; o.textContent = val;
        if ((fromInput.value && fromInput.value.toLowerCase() === key) || hadSelection === val) o.selected = true;
        sel.appendChild(o);
      });
    }

    repopulateFromDatalist();
    if (!dl._mobileSelectSyncAttached) {
      new MutationObserver(repopulateFromDatalist).observe(dl, { childList: true });
      dl._mobileSelectSyncAttached = true;
    }
  }

  function initAll(){ document.querySelectorAll('input[list]').forEach(buildMobileSelect); }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initAll);
  else initAll();

  new MutationObserver(initAll).observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
})();

// mirror visible phone into hidden sdphone for legacy paths
(function(){
  const phone = document.getElementById("phone");
  const sdphone = document.getElementById("sdphone");
  if (phone && sdphone) {
    sdphone.removeAttribute('name'); // ensure no duplicate "phone"
    phone.addEventListener("input", function(){ sdphone.value = phone.value; });
  }
})();
</script><script>
// Tab order enforcement
(function () {
  function enforceTabOrder() {
    var form = document.querySelector('form[id^="ctForm_"]');
    if (!form) return;

    var fullName   = form.querySelector('#sdfirstname');
    var workEmail  = form.querySelector('#sdemail');
    var company    = form.querySelector('#company');
    var phoneInput = form.querySelector('#phone') || form.querySelector('#sdphone');
    var submitBtn  = form.querySelector('#schedule_demo');

    var selectedFlag = null;
    if (phoneInput) {
      var itiWrap = phoneInput.closest('.iti');
      if (itiWrap) selectedFlag = itiWrap.querySelector('.iti__selected-flag');
    }

    if (fullName)  fullName.tabIndex  = 1;
    if (workEmail) workEmail.tabIndex = 2;
    if (company)   company.tabIndex   = 3;
    if (selectedFlag) selectedFlag.tabIndex = 4;
    if (phoneInput)  phoneInput.tabIndex   = 5;
    if (submitBtn)   submitBtn.tabIndex    = 6;

    var allowed = new Set([fullName, workEmail, company, selectedFlag, phoneInput, submitBtn].filter(Boolean));
    form.querySelectorAll('a[href], button, input, select, textarea').forEach(function (el) {
      if (allowed.has(el)) return;
      el.tabIndex = -1;
      el.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    });

    ['#consent_checkbox','button[name="close-modal"]',
     '#sdphone','#country','.country_hidden',
     '#phone_e164','#phone_dial_code','#phone_country_iso2',
     '#phone_country_name','#phone_number_country'
    ].forEach(function(sel){
      var el = form.querySelector(sel) || document.querySelector(sel);
      if (el) { el.tabIndex = -1; el.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true'); }
    });

    if (selectedFlag) {
      var flagContainer = selectedFlag.parentNode;
      var list = flagContainer && flagContainer.querySelector('.iti__country-list');
      if (list) {
        list.querySelectorAll('[tabindex], li, a, input, button').forEach(function (el) {
          el.tabIndex = -1;
          el.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
        });
      }
    }
  }

  function scheduleEnforce() {
    enforceTabOrder();
    setTimeout(enforceTabOrder, 0);
    setTimeout(enforceTabOrder, 50);
    setTimeout(enforceTabOrder, 200);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', scheduleEnforce);
  else scheduleEnforce();

  var form = document.querySelector('form[id^="ctForm_"]');
  if (form) new MutationObserver(scheduleEnforce).observe(form, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  document.addEventListener('countrychange', scheduleEnforce, true);
})();
</script><style>button#schedule_demo:disabled {background: #50596A !important;opacity: 0.6;cursor: not-allowed;pointer-events: none;}</style><script>

        function getDomain(email) {
            if (!email || email.indexOf("@") === -1) return "";
            return email.split("@").pop().trim().toLowerCase();
        }

        function isBlocked() {
            const domain = getDomain(emailInput.value);
            return domain && freeDomains.has(domain);
        }

        function updateUI(block) {
            submitBtn.disabled = block;
            submitBtn.style.cursor = block ? "not-allowed" : "pointer";
            submitBtn.style.opacity = block ? "0.5" : "1";

            if (block) {
                errorMsg.classList.add("show");
				emailInput.style.border = "1px solid red";

            } else {
                errorMsg.classList.remove("show");
            }
        }
        emailInput.addEventListener("input", function () {
            updateUI(isBlocked());
        });
        submitBtn.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
            if (isBlocked()) {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopImmediatePropagation();
                updateUI(true);
                emailInput.focus();
            }
        });
        form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
            if (isBlocked()) {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopImmediatePropagation();
                updateUI(true);
            }
        }, true);
    }

    if (document.readyState === "loading") {
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initCorporateEmailBlocker);
    } else {
        initCorporateEmailBlocker();
    }
})();
</script> <script>
    $(document).ready(function(){

        var jobs_json = {"status":0,"message":"Invalid Credentials"};
        //Clubbing json response by its parent_department
        var groupedJson = [];
        jobs_json.forEach(function (a) {
            if(a.post_on_carrers_page == 1) {
                if (!this[a.parent_department]) {
                    this[a.parent_department] = {
                        parent_department: a.parent_department,
                        title: [],
                        location_country: [],
                        location_city: [],
                        job_id: []
                    };
                    groupedJson.push(this[a.parent_department]);
                }
                this[a.parent_department].title.push({title: a.title});
                this[a.parent_department].location_country.push({location_country: a.location_country});
                this[a.parent_department].location_city.push({location_city: a.location_city});
                this[a.parent_department].job_id.push({job_id: a.job_id});
            } }, Object.create(null));

       //alert(JSON.stringify(groupedJson));
        //console.log(groupedJson);
       // console.log(jobs_json);
        jobStructure();
        function jobStructure() {
            var HTML = '';
            $.each(groupedJson, function(key, item){
                HTML += '<div class="faqs-ardn">';

                HTML += '<div class="faqs-ardn__head"><span class="accrdn_title">' + item.parent_department + '</span></div>';

                for (var i = 0; i < item.job_id.length; i++) {
                    HTML += '<div class="faqs-ardn__content">';
                    HTML += '<div class="job-dtl-l"> <span class="job-title-wrap">';
                    HTML += '<a href="" class="job-title">' + item.title[i].title + '</a>';
                    HTML += '<span class="job-loc">' + item.location_city[i].location_city + ', ' + item.location_country[i].location_country + '</span></span>';

                    HTML += '<span class="apply_link">';
                    HTML += '<a href="https://clevertap.darwinbox.in/ms/candidate/careers/' + item.job_id[i].job_id + '" class="c-btn" target="_blank">Apply now</a>';
                    HTML += '</span>';
                    HTML += '</div>';

                    HTML += '</div>';
                }
                HTML += '</div>';
            });

            $('.current_jobs_wrapper').append(HTML);
        }

        $('.faqs-ardn__head').on('click', function(){
            var parent = $(this).parent();
            if(parent.hasClass('faqs-ardn--open')) {
                parent.removeClass('faqs-ardn--open');
            } else {
                $('.faqs-ardn').removeClass('faqs-ardn--open');
                parent.addClass('faqs-ardn--open');
            }
        });

    });

} #ctLocGrid .ct-loc-card-meta { display: inline-flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-start; gap: 10px; position: relative; flex: 0 0 auto; } #ctLocGrid .ct-loc-count { position: relative; } #ctLocGrid .ct-loc-label { position: relative; width: fit-content; font-family: "Circular Std-Book", Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-weight: 400; color: #ffffff; font-size: 20px; letter-spacing: -0.6px; } .ct-careers-v2 .ct-benefit img, .ct-careers-v2 .perk-card img, .ct-careers-v2 .ct-benefit .perk-ico, .ct-careers-v2 .perk-card .perk-ico { transform: translate3d(var(--ix), var(--iy), 0) rotate(var(--ir)) scale(1); filter: drop-shadow(0 10px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.28)); } @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { .ct-careers-v2 .ct-benefit img, .ct-careers-v2 .perk-card img, .ct-careers-v2 .ct-benefit .perk-ico, .ct-careers-v2 .perk-card .perk-ico { transition: none; } .ct-careers-v2 .ct-desk-slide .frame-wrapper, .ct-careers-v2 .frame-wrapper { height: auto !important; min-height: 0 !important; } .ct-careers-v2 .ct-desk-slide .div-3, .ct-careers-v2 .div-3 { flex-direction: column !important; overflow: visible !important; display: block !important; -webkit-line-clamp: unset !important; -webkit-box-orient: initial !important; } .ct-careers-v2 .ct-desk-slide .irish-edmondson, .ct-careers-v2 .frame .irish-edmondson { width: 100% !important; } .ct-careers-v2 .ct-desk-slide .div-6, .ct-careers-v2 .frame .div-6 { order: -1; width: 100% !important; min-height: 240px !important; padding-top: 12px !important; } }</style><script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async function () {
        var locGrid = document.getElementById('ctLocGrid');

        if (!locGrid) {
            return;
        }

        var API_URL = 'https://api.kula.ai/v1/job-boards/job-posts';
        var API_TOKEN = '[redacted]';
        var DEFAULT_EXPLORE_LINK = 'https://careers.kula.ai/clevertap';
        var DEFAULT_BG_IMAGE = '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/mumbai.png';
        var LOCATION_CARDS = [
            { key: 'san-francisco', label: 'San Francisco' },
            { key: 'dubai', label: 'Dubai' },
            { key: 'jordan', label: 'Amman' },
            { key: 'saudi-arabia', label: 'Riyadh' },
            { key: 'bangalore', label: 'Bangalore' },
            { key: 'mumbai', label: 'Mumbai' },
            { key: 'delhi', label: 'Gurgaon' },
            { key: 'sao-paulo', label: 'São Paulo' },
            { key: 'mexico', label: 'Mexico' },
            { key: 'colombia', label: 'Bogotá' },
            { key: 'singapore', label: 'Singapore' },
            { key: 'jakarta', label: 'Jakarta' },
            { key: 'philippines', label: 'Metro Manila' },
            { key: 'malaysia', label: 'Kuala Lampur' },
            { key: 'hanoi', label: 'Hanoi' },
            { key: 'ho-chi-minh', label: 'Ho Chi Minh' }
        ];
        var CITY_KEY_ALIASES = {
            bengaluru: 'bangalore',
            'ho-chi-minh-city': 'ho-chi-minh',
            'kuala-lumpur': 'malaysia',
            's-o-paulo': 'sao-paulo'
        };
        var CITY_BG_IMAGES = {
            bangalore: '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/bangalore.png',
            mumbai: '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/mumbai.png',
            delhi: '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/delhi.png',
            'san-francisco': '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/san-francisco.png',
            singapore: '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/singapore.png',
            dubai: '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/dubai.png',
            'sao-paulo': '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/sao-paulo.png',
            mexico: '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/mexico.png',
            jakarta: '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/jakarta.png',
            philippines: '/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Metro-Manila-l.png',
            malaysia: '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/malaysia.png',
            hanoi: '/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Hanoi-l.png',
            'ho-chi-minh': '/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Ho-Chi-minh-l.png',
            jordan: '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/jordan.png',
            'saudi-arabia': '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/saudi-arabia.png',
            colombia: '/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/assets/images/careers/locations/colombia.png'
        };

        var escapeHtml = function (value) {
            return (value || '').toString()
                .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
                .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
                .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
                .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
                .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
        };

        var normalizeCity = function (jobPost) {
            var city = jobPost.location_city
                || jobPost.city
                || (jobPost.location && jobPost.location.city)
                || (jobPost.office && jobPost.office.city);

            if (!city && Array.isArray(jobPost.locations) && jobPost.locations.length > 0) {
                city = jobPost.locations[0].city || jobPost.locations[0].name;
            }

            if (!city && Array.isArray(jobPost.offices) && jobPost.offices.length > 0) {
                city = jobPost.offices[0].city || jobPost.offices[0].name;
            }

            return (city || '').toString().trim();
        };

        var getCityKey = function (city) {
            return (city || '')
                .toString()
                .normalize('NFD')
                .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
                .trim()
                .toLowerCase()
                .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
                .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');
        };

        var getLocationKey = function (city) {
            var rawKey = getCityKey(city);
            return CITY_KEY_ALIASES[rawKey] || rawKey;
        };

        var getExploreBoardLink = function (jobBoardUrl) {
            if (!jobBoardUrl) {
                return '';
            }

            try {
                var parsedUrl = new URL(jobBoardUrl);
                var segments = parsedUrl.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
                if (!segments.length) {
                    return '';
                }

                var lastSegment = segments[segments.length - 1];
                if (/^\d+$/.test(lastSegment)) {
                    segments.pop();
                }

                return parsedUrl.origin + '/' + segments.join('/');
            } catch (error) {
                return '';
            }
        };

        var getJobPosts = function (responseData) {
            if (Array.isArray(responseData)) {
                return responseData;
            }

            if (responseData && Array.isArray(responseData.data)) {
                return responseData.data;
            }

            if (responseData && Array.isArray(responseData.job_posts)) {
                return responseData.job_posts;
            }

            return [];
        };

        var isOpenJob = function (jobPost) {
            var blockedStatus = {
                closed: true,
                internal: true,
                draft: true,
                archived: true
            };

            var status = (jobPost.status || '').toString().toLowerCase();
            return !blockedStatus[status];
        };

        var extractCities = function (jobPost) {
            if (Array.isArray(jobPost.offices) && jobPost.offices.length > 0) {
                return jobPost.offices.map(function (office) {
                    return (office.city || office.name || '').toString().trim();
                }).filter(Boolean);
            }

            var city = normalizeCity(jobPost);
            return city ? [city] : [];
        };

        var fetchAllJobPosts = async function () {
            var allJobs = [];
            var page = 1;
            var totalPages = 1;

            while (page <= totalPages) {
                var response = await fetch(API_URL + '?page=' + page, {
                    method: 'GET',
                    headers: {
                        'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + API_TOKEN
                    }
                });

                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error('KULA API request failed with status ' + response.status + ' on page ' + page);
                }

                var responseData = await response.json();
                var pageJobs = getJobPosts(responseData);
                allJobs = allJobs.concat(pageJobs);

                totalPages = (responseData && responseData.meta && Number(responseData.meta.total_pages)) || 1;
                page += 1;
            }

            return allJobs;
        };

        var renderState = function (message) {
            locGrid.innerHTML = '<div class="ct-loc-frame"><p class="ct-loc-message">' + escapeHtml(message) + '</p></div>';
        };

        var getCardBackgroundStyle = function (locationKey) {
            var imageValue = CITY_BG_IMAGES[locationKey] || DEFAULT_BG_IMAGE;
            var imageList = Array.isArray(imageValue) ? imageValue : [imageValue];
            var imageCss = imageList.filter(Boolean).map(function (imagePath) {
                return 'url(\'' + escapeHtml(imagePath) + '\')';
            }).join(', ');

            if (!imageCss) {
                return '';
            }

            var sizeCss = 'cover';
            var positionCss = 'center';
            var repeatCss = 'no-repeat';

            if (imageList.length > 1) {
                sizeCss = imageList.map(function () {
                    return '50% 100%';
                }).join(', ');
                positionCss = imageList.map(function (_, index) {
                    if (index === 0) {
                        return 'left center';
                    }

                    if (index === imageList.length - 1) {
                        return 'right center';
                    }

                    return 'center center';
                }).join(', ');
                repeatCss = imageList.map(function () {
                    return 'no-repeat';
                }).join(', ');
            }

            return ' style="--ct-loc-bg:' + imageCss + ';--ct-loc-bg-size:' + sizeCss + ';--ct-loc-bg-position:' + positionCss + ';--ct-loc-bg-repeat:' + repeatCss + ';"';
        };

        var renderCards = function (locationDataByKey, globalExploreLink) {
            var cardsHtml = LOCATION_CARDS.map(function (locationCard) {
                var cardData = locationDataByKey[locationCard.key] || {};
                var openings = cardData.openings || 0;
                var exploreLink = cardData.link || globalExploreLink || DEFAULT_EXPLORE_LINK;
                var backgroundStyle = getCardBackgroundStyle(locationCard.key);
                var countHtml = openings > 0
                    ? '<div class="ct-loc-count">' + openings + '</div>'
                    : '';

                return ''
                    + '<article class="ct-loc-card">'
                    + '<a class="ct-loc-card-link" href="' + escapeHtml(exploreLink) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">'
                    + '<span class="ct-loc-card-bg"' + backgroundStyle + ' aria-hidden="true"></span>'
                    + '<div class="ct-loc-card-content">'
                    + '<div class="ct-loc-card-meta">'
                    + countHtml
                    + '<div class="ct-loc-label">Open Positions</div>'
                    + '</div>'
                    + '<div class="ct-loc-arrow" aria-hidden="true">→</div>'
                    + '</div>'
                    + '</a>'
                    + '<h3 class="ct-loc-city">' + escapeHtml(locationCard.label) + '</h3>'
                    + '</article>';
            }).join('');

            locGrid.innerHTML = ''
                + '<section class="ct-loc-frame">'
                + '<div class="ct-loc-header">'
                + '<div class="ct-loc-kicker">LOCATIONS</div>'
                + '<p class="ct-loc-title"><span class="ct-loc-title-main">Where the </span><span class="ct-loc-title-highlight">magic</span><span class="ct-loc-title-main"> happens</span></p>'
                + '</div>'
                + '<div class="ct-loc-grid">' + cardsHtml + '</div>'
                + '</section>';
        };

        renderState('Loading open locations...');

        try {
            var jobPosts = await fetchAllJobPosts();

            var locationData = jobPosts.reduce(function (acc, jobPost) {
                if (!isOpenJob(jobPost)) {
                    return acc;
                }

                var cities = extractCities(jobPost);
                if (!cities.length) {
                    return acc;
                }

                var boardLinkFromJob = getExploreBoardLink(jobPost.job_board_url);
                cities.forEach(function (city) {
                    var locationKey = getLocationKey(city);
                    if (!acc[locationKey]) {
                        acc[locationKey] = {
                            openings: 0,
                            link: ''
                        };
                    }

                    acc[locationKey].openings += 1;
                    if (!acc[locationKey].link && boardLinkFromJob) {
                        acc[locationKey].link = boardLinkFromJob;
                    }
                });

                return acc;
            }, {});

            var globalExploreLink = '';
            for (var i = 0; i < jobPosts.length; i++) {
                globalExploreLink = getExploreBoardLink(jobPosts[i].job_board_url);
                if (globalExploreLink) {
                    break;
                }
            }

            renderCards(locationData, globalExploreLink);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to load KULA locations:', error);
            renderCards({}, DEFAULT_EXPLORE_LINK);
        }
    });
</script><script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        var cards = document.querySelectorAll('.ct-careers-v2 .ct-benefit, .ct-careers-v2 .perk-card');

        if (!cards.length) {
            return;
        }

        cards.forEach(function (card) {
            var onMove = function (event) {
                var rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
                var x = event.clientX - rect.left;
                var y = event.clientY - rect.top;
                var dx = ((x / rect.width) - 0.5) * 12;
                var dy = ((y / rect.height) - 0.5) * 12;
                var rotate = ((x / rect.width) - 0.5) * 10;

                card.style.setProperty('--x', x + 'px');
                card.style.setProperty('--y', y + 'px');
                card.style.setProperty('--ix', dx.toFixed(2) + 'px');
                card.style.setProperty('--iy', dy.toFixed(2) + 'px');
                card.style.setProperty('--ir', rotate.toFixed(2) + 'deg');
                card.classList.add('spotlight');
            };

            var onLeave = function () {
                card.style.setProperty('--x', '50%');
                card.style.setProperty('--y', '50%');
                card.style.setProperty('--ix', '0px');
                card.style.setProperty('--iy', '0px');
                card.style.setProperty('--ir', '0deg');
                card.classList.remove('spotlight');
            };

            card.addEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
            card.addEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
            card.addEventListener('blur', onLeave, true);
        });
    });

		(function () {
			function initLangSwitchers() {
			var switchers = document.querySelectorAll('.ct-lang-switch');
			if (!switchers.length) return;

			function closeAll() {
				switchers.forEach(function (switcher) {
					switcher.classList.remove('is-open');
					var toggle = switcher.querySelector('.ct-lang-toggle');
					if (toggle) toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
				});
			}

			function ctLangSetCookie( lang ) {
				var exp = new Date();
				exp.setTime( exp.getTime() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 );
				document.cookie = 'ct_lang_pref=' + encodeURIComponent( lang ) +
					'; expires=' + exp.toUTCString() +
					'; path=/; SameSite=Lax';
			}

			var closeTimer = null;

			switchers.forEach(function (switcher) {
				var toggle = switcher.querySelector('.ct-lang-toggle');
				if (!toggle) return;

				// Hover opens on desktop (mouseenter is a no-op on touch devices).
				switcher.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () {
					clearTimeout(closeTimer);
					closeAll();
					switcher.classList.add('is-open');
					toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
				});
				switcher.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () {
					closeTimer = setTimeout(function () {
						switcher.classList.remove('is-open');
						toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
					}, 180);
				});

				// Click/tap opens on mobile (pointer devices ignore this since hover already opened it).
				toggle.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
					e.preventDefault();
					e.stopPropagation();
					var willOpen = !switcher.classList.contains('is-open');
					closeAll();
					switcher.classList.toggle('is-open', willOpen);
					toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', willOpen ? 'true' : 'false');
				});

				// Explicit language pick overrides any geo-modal cookie.
				switcher.querySelectorAll('.ct-lang-option').forEach(function (link) {
					link.addEventListener('click', function () {
						var href = link.getAttribute('href') || '/';
						var lang = 'en';
						if ( /^\/tr(\/|$)/.test(href) )      lang = 'tr';
						else if ( /^\/es(\/|$)/.test(href) ) lang = 'es';
						else if ( /^\/pt(\/|$)/.test(href) ) lang = 'pt';
						ctLangSetCookie( lang );
					});
				});
			});

			document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
				var inside = false;
				switchers.forEach(function (switcher) {
					if (switcher.contains(e.target)) inside = true;
				});
				if (!inside) closeAll();
			});
			document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
				if (e.key === 'Escape' || e.keyCode === 27) closeAll();
			});
			}

			if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
				document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initLangSwitchers);
			} else {
				initLangSwitchers();
			}
		})();
	</script> <li class="no-blue"> <a href="https://eu1.dashboard.clevertap.com/" target="_blank" style="position: relative;top: 1px;">Log In</a> </li> <li> <a href="javascript:;" class="c-btn medium-btn class="ctzen-life__slide ctzen-life__slide--6"><img src="https://clevertap.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/SF-Team-Lunch-2.webp" alt="Life at CleverTap team culture moment 12" loading="lazy" href="https://careers.kula.ai/clevertap">Explore Careers </a> </div> </div> <script>
					(function () {
						var root = document.querySelector(".ctzen-life");
						if (!root) return;

						var viewport = root.querySelector("[data-slider-viewport]");
						var track = root.querySelector("[data-slider-track]");
						if (!viewport || !track) return;

						var reducedMotion = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
						var originalCount = 13;
						var autoSpeed = reducedMotion ? 0 : 0.99; // smaller = slower
						var currentX = 0;
						var setWidth = 0;
						var isDragging = false;
						var isHovered = false;
						var startX = 0;
						var dragStartX = 0;
						var lastTs = 0;
						var rafId = 0;

						function getGap() {
							return parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(track).gap) || 0;
						}

						function measure() {
							var slides = track.querySelectorAll(".ctzen-life__slide");
							if (!slides.length) return;

							var gap = getGap();
							var width = 0;

							for (var i = 0; i < originalCount; i++) {
								if (!slides[i]) break;
								width += slides[i].getBoundingClientRect().width;
								if (i < originalCount - 1) width += gap;
							}

							setWidth = width;
						}

						function normalizeX() {
							if (!setWidth) return;

							while (currentX <= -setWidth) currentX += setWidth;
							while (currentX > 0) currentX -= setWidth;
						}

						function render() {
							track.style.transform = "translate3d(" + currentX + "px,0,0)";
						}

						function animate(ts) {
							if (!lastTs) lastTs = ts;
							var delta = (ts - lastTs) / 16.67;
							lastTs = ts;

							if (!isDragging && !isHovered && !reducedMotion) {
								currentX -= autoSpeed * delta;
								normalizeX();
								render();
							}

							rafId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
						}

						function pointerDown(e) {
							isDragging = true;
							viewport.classList.add("is-dragging");
							startX = e.clientX;
							dragStartX = currentX;

							if (viewport.setPointerCapture && e.pointerId !== undefined) {
								viewport.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
							}
						}

						function pointerMove(e) {
							if (!isDragging) return;
							var dx = e.clientX - startX;
							currentX = dragStartX + dx;
							normalizeX();
							render();
						}

						function pointerUp(e) {
							if (!isDragging) return;
							isDragging = false;
							viewport.classList.remove("is-dragging");

							if (
								viewport.releasePointerCapture &&
								e.pointerId !== undefined &&
								viewport.hasPointerCapture &&
								viewport.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId)
							) {
								viewport.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);
							}
						}

						viewport.addEventListener("pointerdown", pointerDown);
						viewport.addEventListener("pointermove", pointerMove);
						viewport.addEventListener("pointerup", pointerUp);
						viewport.addEventListener("pointercancel", pointerUp);
						viewport.addEventListener("lostpointercapture", pointerUp);

						viewport.addEventListener("mouseenter", function () {
							isHovered = true;
						});

						viewport.addEventListener("mouseleave", function () {
							isHovered = false;
						});

						window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
							measure();
							normalizeX();
							render();
						});

						window.addEventListener("load", function () {
							measure();
							normalizeX();
							render();
						});

						setTimeout(function () {
							measure();
							normalizeX();
							render();
						}, 250);

						setTimeout(function () {
							measure();
							normalizeX();
							render();
						}, 900);

						measure();
						render();
						rafId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
					})();
				</script> </section> <div class="frame"> <div class="div"> <p class="from-the-ctzen-s"> <span class="text-wrapper">From the </span> <span class="span">CTzen&#8217;s Desk</span> </p> <div class="ct-desk-nav"> <button class="ct-desk-arrow prev" aria-label="Previous slide"> <span>&larr;</span> </button> <button class="ct-desk-arrow next" aria-label="Next slide"> <span>&rarr;</span> </button> </div> </div> <div class="group ct-desk-slider"> <div class="ct-desk-track"> <div class="ct-desk-slide"> <div class="frame-wrapper blue"> <div class="div-3"> <div class="div-4"> <div class="icon-park-quote"> <img decoding="async" src="https://clevertap.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/test-3.svg" alt="Quote icon"> </div> <div class="div-5"> <p class="p"> When I joined CleverTap, I was intrigued by the strength of the product and the opportunity to help build out the NAM team. 				document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
					const slider = document.querySelector(".ct-desk-slider");
					const track = slider.querySelector(".ct-desk-track");
					const slides = slider.querySelectorAll(".ct-desk-slide");
					const prevBtn = document.querySelector(".ct-desk-arrow.prev");
					const nextBtn = document.querySelector(".ct-desk-arrow.next");

					let currentIndex = 0;

					function updateSlider() {
						track.style.transform = `translateX(-${currentIndex * 100}%)`;
					}

					prevBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
						currentIndex = currentIndex === 0 ? slides.length - 1 : currentIndex - 1;
						updateSlider();
					});

					nextBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
						currentIndex = currentIndex === slides.length - 1 ? 0 : currentIndex + 1;
						updateSlider();
					});

					// Optional swipe support for mobile/tablet
					let startX = 0;
					let endX = 0;

					slider.addEventListener("touchstart", function (e) {
						startX = e.touches[0].clientX;
					}, { passive: true });

					slider.addEventListener("touchend", function (e) {
						endX = e.changedTouches[0].clientX;
						const diff = startX - endX;

						if (Math.abs(diff) > 50) {
							if (diff > 0) {
								currentIndex = currentIndex === slides.length - 1 ? 0 : currentIndex + 1;
							} else {
								currentIndex = currentIndex === 0 ? slides.length - 1 : currentIndex - 1;
							}
							updateSlider();
						}
					}, { passive: true });
				});

				let finalRating = 4.4;
				let ratingDataReady = false;
				let ratingInView = false;
				let ratingAnimationStarted = false;
				function maybeStartRatingAnimations() {
					if (ratingAnimationStarted || !ratingDataReady || !ratingInView) return;
					ratingAnimationStarted = true;
					if (ratingCard) ratingCard.classList.add('is-animated');
					animateGlassdoorRating(finalRating);
				}
				function animateGlassdoorRating(targetRating){
					const ratingNumber = document.getElementById('ctGlassdoorRatingNumber');
					if(!ratingNumber) return;
					const duration = 2000;
					const startTime = performance.now();
					function updateRating(currentTime){
						const elapsed = currentTime - startTime;
						const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
						const eased = progress * (2 - progress);
						ratingNumber.textContent = (eased * targetRating).toFixed(1);
						if(progress < 1) requestAnimationFrame(updateRating);
					}
					requestAnimationFrame(updateRating);
				}
				async function hydrateEmployerRating(){
					try{
						const res = await fetch('/wp-json/ct/v1/glassdoor-rating', { credentials:'same-origin' });
						if(res.ok){
							const data = await res.json();
							if (data.rating) finalRating = Number(data.rating);
							if (data.recommend) document.getElementById('ctRecommend').textContent = `${data.recommend} Recommend`;
							if (data.culture) document.getElementById('ctCulture').textContent = `${data.culture} culture`;
							if (data.diversity) document.getElementById('ctDiversity').textContent = `${data.diversity} Diversity`;
							if (data.ceo_approve) document.getElementById('ctCEOApprove').textContent = `${data.ceo_approve} CEO Approved`;
						}
					} catch(err) {
						console.warn('rating endpoint unavailable', err);
					} finally {
						ratingDataReady = true;
						maybeStartRatingAnimations();
					}
				}
				if (ratingCard) {
					const ratingObs = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
						entries.forEach((entry) => {
							if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
							ratingInView = true;
							maybeStartRatingAnimations();
							ratingObs.unobserve(ratingCard); // run once
						});
					}, { threshold: 0.35 });
					ratingObs.observe(ratingCard);
				}
				hydrateEmployerRating();

				}

	(function () {
		const slider = document.querySelector('[data-slider]');
		if (!slider) return;

		const track = slider.querySelector('.ctzens-desk__track');
		const slides = Array.from(slider.querySelectorAll('.ctzens-desk__slide'));
		const prevBtn = document.querySelector('.ctzens-desk__arrow--prev');
		const nextBtn = document.querySelector('.ctzens-desk__arrow--next');
		const dotsWrap = slider.querySelector('.ctzens-desk__dots');

		let currentIndex = 0;
		let startX = 0;
		let currentTranslate = 0;
		let isDragging = false;
		let movedBy = 0;

		slides.forEach((_, index) => {
			const dot = document.createElement('button');
			dot.className = 'ctzens-desk__dot' + (index === 0 ? ' is-active' : '');
			dot.type = 'button';
			dot.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Go to slide ' + (index + 1));
			dot.addEventListener('click', () => goToSlide(index));
			dotsWrap.appendChild(dot);
		});

		const dots = Array.from(dotsWrap.querySelectorAll('.ctzens-desk__dot'));

		function updateSlider() {
			const offset = -currentIndex * 100;
			track.style.transform = `translate3d(${offset}%, 0, 0)`;

			slides.forEach((slide, i) => {
				const isActive = i === currentIndex;
				slide.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);
				slide.setAttribute('aria-hidden', isActive ? 'false' : 'true');
			});

			dots.forEach((dot, i) => {
				dot.classList.toggle('is-active', i === currentIndex);
			});
		}

		function goToSlide(index) {
			currentIndex = (index + slides.length) % slides.length;
			updateSlider();
		}

		function nextSlide() {
			goToSlide(currentIndex + 1);
		}

		function prevSlide() {
			goToSlide(currentIndex - 1);
		}

		if (nextBtn) nextBtn.addEventListener('click', nextSlide);
		if (prevBtn) prevBtn.addEventListener('click', prevSlide);

		slider.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
			if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') nextSlide();
			if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') prevSlide();
		});

		slider.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');

		function getPositionX(e) {
			return e.type.includes('mouse') ? e.pageX : e.touches[0].clientX;
		}

		function touchStart(e) {
			isDragging = true;
			startX = getPositionX(e);
			movedBy = 0;
			track.style.transition = 'none';
		}

		function touchMove(e) {
			if (!isDragging) return;
			const currentX = getPositionX(e);
			movedBy = currentX - startX;
			const percentageMove = (movedBy / slider.offsetWidth) * 100;
			currentTranslate = -currentIndex * 100 + percentageMove;
			track.style.transform = `translate3d(${currentTranslate}%, 0, 0)`;
		}

		function touchEnd() {
			if (!isDragging) return;
			isDragging = false;
			track.style.transition = '';

			const threshold = slider.offsetWidth * 0.12;
			if (movedBy < -threshold) {
				nextSlide();
			} else if (movedBy > threshold) {
				prevSlide();
			} else {
				updateSlider();
			}
		}

		slider.addEventListener('touchstart', touchStart, { passive: true });
		slider.addEventListener('touchmove', touchMove, { passive: true });
		slider.addEventListener('touchend', touchEnd);

		slider.addEventListener('mousedown', touchStart);
		slider.addEventListener('mousemove', touchMove);
		slider.addEventListener('mouseup', touchEnd);
		slider.addEventListener('mouseleave', touchEnd);

		window.addEventListener('resize', updateSlider);

		updateSlider();
	})();
</script><script>
	(function () {
		const perkCards = document.querySelectorAll('.ct-careers-v2 .ct-benefit');
		if (!perkCards.length) return;

		perkCards.forEach((card) => {
			card.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
				const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
				const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
				const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
				card.style.setProperty('--x', x + 'px');
				card.style.setProperty('--y', y + 'px');
			});

			card.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => {
				card.style.setProperty('--x', '50%');
				card.style.setProperty('--y', '50%');
			});
		});
	})();

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
  // Find all buttons
  const buttons = document.querySelectorAll('button, a');

  buttons.forEach(function (btn) {
    // Match the button with exact text
    if (btn.textContent.trim().toLowerCase() === 'download the guide') {
      btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
        const form = document.querySelector('form[id^="ctForm_"]');
        const formId = form ? form.id.replace('ctForm_', '') : '';

        window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
        window.dataLayer.push({
          event: 'guide_signup_form_view',
          form_id: formId,
          page_url: window.location.href
        });
      });
    }
  });

  function getToggleForList(list) {
    return list && list.id
      ? document.querySelector('.footer-collapsible-toggle[aria-controls="' + list.id + '"]')
      : null;
  }

  function unlockExpandedAncestors(list) {
    var parent = list ? list.parentElement : null;
    while (parent) {
      if (parent.classList && parent.classList.contains('footer-collapsible-links')) {
        var parentToggle = getToggleForList(parent);
        if (parentToggle && parentToggle.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true') {
          parent.style.maxHeight = 'none';
          parent.style.overflow = 'visible';
        }
      }
      parent = parent.parentElement;
    }
  }

  function openCollapsibleList(list) {
    list.hidden = false;
    list.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    list.style.maxHeight = 'none';
    var fullHeight = list.scrollHeight;
    list.style.maxHeight = '0px';
    list.style.opacity = '0';
    // Force reflow so the browser animates from 0 -> full height.
    void list.offsetHeight;
    list.style.maxHeight = fullHeight + 'px';
    list.style.opacity = '1';
    unlockExpandedAncestors(list);

    window.setTimeout(function () {
      var toggle = getToggleForList(list);
      if (toggle && toggle.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true') {
        list.style.maxHeight = 'none';
        list.style.overflow = 'visible';
        unlockExpandedAncestors(list);
      }
    }, 320);
  }

  function closeCollapsibleList(list) {
    list.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    if (list.style.maxHeight === 'none' || !list.style.maxHeight) {
      list.style.maxHeight = list.scrollHeight + 'px';
      void list.offsetHeight;
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(function () {
      list.style.maxHeight = '0px';
      list.style.opacity = '0';
      unlockExpandedAncestors(list);
    });
  }

  const footerDropdownButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.footer-collapsible-toggle');

  footerDropdownButtons.forEach(function (toggle) {
    toggle.addEventListener('click', function () {
      const targetId = this.getAttribute('aria-controls');
      const targetList = targetId ? document.getElementById(targetId) : null;

      if (!targetList) {
        return;
      }

      const isExpanded = this.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
      this.setAttribute('aria-expanded', (!isExpanded).toString());
      if (isExpanded) {
        closeCollapsibleList(targetList);
      } else {
        openCollapsibleList(targetList);
      }
    });
  });

  const collapsibleLists = document.querySelectorAll('.footer-collapsible-links');
  collapsibleLists.forEach(function (list) {
    list.hidden = false;
    list.style.maxHeight = '0px';
    list.style.opacity = '0';
    list.style.overflow = 'hidden';
  });
});

(function () {
    var COOKIE       = 'ct_lang_pref';
    var DAYS         = 365;
    var overlay      = document.getElementById('ct-geo-lang-overlay');
    var currentLang  = "en";
    var langDisplay  = {"en":"English","tr":"Turkish","es":"Spanish","pt":"Brazilian Portuguese"};
    var langUrls     = {"en":"\/","tr":"\/tr\/","es":"\/es\/","pt":"\/pt\/"};
    var titleEl      = document.getElementById('ct-geo-lang-title');
    var subtitleEl   = document.getElementById('ct-geo-lang-subtitle');
    var countryEl    = document.getElementById('ct-geo-lang-country');
    var flagEl       = document.getElementById('ct-geo-lang-flag');
    var acceptBtn    = document.getElementById('ct-geo-lang-accept');
    var declineBtn   = document.getElementById('ct-geo-lang-decline');
    var closeBtn     = document.getElementById('ct-geo-lang-close');

    function getCookie( name ) {
        var m = document.cookie.match( new RegExp( '(?:^|;\\s*)' + name + '=([^;]*)' ) );
        return m ? decodeURIComponent( m[1] ) : null;
    }

    function setCookie( name, value ) {
        var exp = new Date();
        exp.setTime( exp.getTime() + DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 );
        document.cookie = name + '=' + encodeURIComponent( value ) +
            '; expires=' + exp.toUTCString() +
            '; path=/; SameSite=Lax';
    }

    function show() {
        if ( overlay ) {
            overlay.classList.remove( 'is-hidden' );
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        }
    }

    function hide() {
        if ( overlay ) {
            overlay.classList.add( 'is-hidden' );
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
        }
    }

    function getFlagEmoji(code) {
        if (!code || typeof code !== 'string' || !/^[A-Za-z]{2}$/.test(code)) {
            return '';
        }
        var upper = code.toUpperCase();
        var chars = upper.split('');
        return String.fromCodePoint(chars[0].charCodeAt(0) + 127397) +
               String.fromCodePoint(chars[1].charCodeAt(0) + 127397);
    }

    function resolveLanguageUrl(langCode) {
        if (langUrls && langUrls[langCode]) {
            return langUrls[langCode];
        }
        return langCode === 'en' ? '/' : '/' + langCode + '/';
    }

    function applyModalCopy(countryName, countryCode, suggestedLang) {
        var suggestedLabel = langDisplay[suggestedLang] || suggestedLang.toUpperCase();
        var currentLabel = langDisplay[currentLang] || 'current';
        var safeCountryName = countryName || countryCode || 'your location';
        var flag = getFlagEmoji(countryCode);

        if (titleEl) {
            titleEl.textContent = "You're visiting from " + safeCountryName;
        }
        if (subtitleEl) {
            subtitleEl.textContent = 'We have a ' + suggestedLabel + ' version of our site. Would you like to switch for a better experience?';
        }
        if (countryEl) {
            countryEl.textContent = safeCountryName;
        }
        if (flagEl) {
            flagEl.textContent = flag;
            flagEl.style.display = flag ? 'inline' : 'none';
        }
        if (acceptBtn) {
            acceptBtn.textContent = 'Switch to ' + suggestedLabel + ' site';
            acceptBtn.setAttribute('data-lang', suggestedLang);
            acceptBtn.setAttribute('href', resolveLanguageUrl(suggestedLang));
        }
        if (declineBtn) {
            declineBtn.textContent = 'Stay on ' + currentLabel + ' site';
        }
    }

    function shouldShowForLang(suggestedLang) {
        if (!suggestedLang) {
            return currentLang !== 'en' ? 'en' : '';
        }
        if (suggestedLang === currentLang) {
            return '';
        }
        return suggestedLang;
    }

    function removeOverlayNode() {
        if (overlay && overlay.parentNode) {
            overlay.parentNode.removeChild(overlay);
        }
    }

    function trackGeoLangDecision(action, reason, extra) {
        if (!window.dataLayer || typeof window.dataLayer.push !== 'function') {
            return;
        }
        var payload = {
            event: 'ct_geo_lang_decision',
            ct_geo_lang_action: action,
            ct_geo_lang_reason: reason || '',
            ct_geo_lang_current_lang: currentLang
        };
        if (extra && typeof extra === 'object') {
            for (var key in extra) {
                if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(extra, key)) {
                    payload[key] = extra[key];
                }
            }
        }
        window.dataLayer.push(payload);
    }

    function fetchGeoLangPayload(cb) {
        var params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        var debugCountry = params.get('ct_test_country');
        var endpoint = '/wp-json/ct/v1/geo-lang?ts=' + Date.now();
        if (debugCountry) {
            endpoint += '&ct_test_country=' + encodeURIComponent(debugCountry);
        }

        fetch(endpoint, {
            method: 'GET',
            credentials: 'same-origin',
            cache: 'no-store'
        })
        .then(function(res) { return res.ok ? res.json() : null; })
        .then(function(data) { cb(data); })
        .catch(function() { cb(null); });
    }

    // If preference already stored, remove the node entirely and stop.
    if ( getCookie( COOKIE ) ) {
        removeOverlayNode();
        return;
    }

    if (typeof window.fetch !== 'function') {
        removeOverlayNode();
        return;
    }

    fetchGeoLangPayload(function(payload) {
        if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object') {
            removeOverlayNode();
            return;
        }

        var countryCode = String(payload.countryCode || '').toUpperCase();
        var countryName = String(payload.countryName || '').trim();
        var suggestedLangRaw = String(payload.suggestedLang || '').toLowerCase();
        var effectiveSuggestedLang = shouldShowForLang(suggestedLangRaw);

        if (new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).has('ct_geo_debug') && window.console && typeof window.console.log === 'function') {
            window.console.log('CT GEO DEBUG', {
                countryCode: countryCode,
                countryName: countryName,
                suggestedLang: suggestedLangRaw,
                effectiveSuggestedLang: effectiveSuggestedLang,
                currentLang: currentLang
            });
        }

        if (!effectiveSuggestedLang) {
            removeOverlayNode();
            return;
        }

        applyModalCopy(countryName, countryCode, effectiveSuggestedLang);
        trackGeoLangDecision('shown', 'eligible', {
            ct_geo_lang_country_code: countryCode,
            ct_geo_lang_country_name: countryName,
            ct_geo_lang_suggested_lang: effectiveSuggestedLang
        });
        show();
    });

    if ( acceptBtn ) {
        acceptBtn.addEventListener( 'click', function () {
            var suggested = acceptBtn.getAttribute('data-lang') || currentLang;
            setCookie( COOKIE, suggested );
            trackGeoLangDecision('accepted', 'switch_click', {
                ct_geo_lang_suggested_lang: suggested
            });
        } );
    }
    if ( declineBtn ) {
        declineBtn.addEventListener( 'click', function () {
            setCookie( COOKIE, currentLang );
            trackGeoLangDecision('declined', 'stay_click');
            hide();
        } );
    }
    if ( closeBtn ) {
        closeBtn.addEventListener( 'click', function () {
            setCookie( COOKIE, currentLang );
            trackGeoLangDecision('declined', 'close_icon');
            hide();
        } );
    }
    if ( overlay ) {
        overlay.addEventListener( 'click', function ( e ) {
            if ( e.target === overlay ) {
                setCookie( COOKIE, currentLang );
                trackGeoLangDecision('declined', 'backdrop_click');
                hide();
            }
        } );
    }
    document.addEventListener( 'keydown', function ( e ) {
        if ( ( e.key === 'Escape' || e.keyCode === 27 ) &&
             overlay && ! overlay.classList.contains( 'is-hidden' ) ) {
            setCookie( COOKIE, currentLang );
            trackGeoLangDecision('declined', 'escape_key');
            hide();
        }
    } );
})();

</script><script data-wp-strategy="defer" defer id="ct-ga-gtm-tracking-js" src="https://clevertap.com/wp-content/themes/clevertap2023/js/ct-ga-gtm-tracking.js?ver=1792754293"></script><script type="text/javascript">

    var intlOpts = Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions(),

    if (region === 'LATAM') {
        $(".footer.blogList-footer-social").html('<div class="footer blogList-footer-social lhs-cta-blog MT40" style="display: none;"><p class = "CTA-text-blog">Os principais insights e as melhores práticas do setor entregues em sua caixa de emails.</p><button id="subscribeBtn" name="submit" type="button" class="open-modal c-btn" data-modal="subscribe">Inscreva-se</button> </div>');
    }
    /* Currency logic for BFS starts */
    if (region === 'India
